interactions), i.e., negative means interactions are better\ndifferences = np.array(fold_scores_raw) - np.array(fold_scores_interactions)\nprint(f\"\\nDifferences (Raw - Interactions): {differences}\")\n\n# Perform paired t-test\nt_stat, p_value = stats.ttest_rel(fold_scores_raw, fold_scores_interactions)\nprint(f\"\\nPaired t-test results:\")\nprint(f\"t-statistic: {t_stat:.6f}\")\nprint(f\"p-value: {p_value:.6f}\")\n\ninteraction_helps = t_stat > 2.0\nprint(f\"Interaction helps (t > 2.0): {interaction_helps}\")\n\npaired_t_stat = t_stat\n"},"partialResult":{"content":[{"type":"text","text":"1    | 0.387130    | 0.377645"}],"details":{"status":"ok"}}}

1) - 1\n            \n            X_train_quant = np.round(X_train_sparse / (np.abs(X_train_sparse).max() + 1e-8) * max_val).astype(np.float64)\n            X_test_quant = np.round(X_test_sparse / (np.abs(X_test_sparse).max() + 1e-8) * max_val).astype(np.float64)\n            \n            # Train model on sparse + quantized data\n            model = LogisticRegression(C=1.0/10.0, solver='lbfgs', max_iter=1000)\n            model.fit(X_train_quant, y_train)\n            y_pred_proba = model.predict_proba(X_test_quant)[:, 1]\n            brier = brier_score_loss(y_test, y_pred_proba)\n            config_scores.append(brier)\n        \n        mean_score = np.mean(config_scores)\n        results_config[(sparsity, bits)] = mean_score\n        print(f\"Sparsity {sparsity}% + {bits} bits: Mean Brier = {mean_score:.6f}\")\n\n# Find the configuration that retains the most dense-model performance\n# (i.e., has the closest score to the dense baseline)\nbest_config = None\nbest_performance_retention = -np.inf\n\nfor config, score in results_config.items():\n    # How close to dense baseline? (lower is better, so we want smallest difference)\n    difference = abs(score - dense_baseline)\n    retention = -difference  # Negate to maximize retention\n    \n    if retention > best_performance_retention:\n        best_performance_retention = retention\n        best_config = config\n\nsparsity_best, bits_best = best_config\nprint(f\"\\nBest configuration: {sparsity_best}% sparsity + {bits_best} bits\")\nprint(f\"Brier score: {results_config[best_config]:.6f}\")\nprint(f\"Dense baseline: {dense_baseline:.6f}\")\nprint(f\"Difference: {abs(results_config[best_config] - dense_baseline):.6f}\")\n"}}}}

1) - 1\n            \n            X_train_quant = np.round(X_train_sparse / (np.abs(X_train_sparse).max() + 1e-8) * max_val).astype(np.float64)\n            X_test_quant = np.round(X_test_sparse / (np.abs(X_test_sparse).max() + 1e-8) * max_val).astype(np.float64)\n            \n            # Train model on sparse + quantized data\n            model = LogisticRegression(C=1.0/10.0, solver='lbfgs', max_iter=1000)\n            model.fit(X_train_quant, y_train)\n            y_pred_proba = model.predict_proba(X_test_quant)[:, 1]\n            brier = brier_score_loss(y_test, y_pred_proba)\n            config_scores.append(brier)\n        \n        mean_score = np.mean(config_scores)\n        results_config[(sparsity, bits)] = mean_score\n        print(f\"Sparsity {sparsity}% + {bits} bits: Mean Brier = {mean_score:.6f}\")\n\n# Find the configuration that retains the most dense-model performance\n# (i.e., has the closest score to the dense baseline)\nbest_config = None\nbest_performance_retention = -np.inf\n\nfor config, score in results_config.items():\n    # How close to dense baseline? sparsity) / 100)\n            \n            # Select top features based on absolute weight magnitude from a quick model\n            model_quick = LogisticRegression(C=1.0/10.0, solver='lbfgs', max_iter=1000)\n            model_quick.fit(X_train, y_train)\n            feature_importance = np.abs(model_quick.coef_[0])\n            top_features = np.argsort(feature_importance)[-n_keep:]\n            \n            X_train_sparse = X_train[:, top_features]\n            X_test_sparse = X_test[:, top_features]\n            \n            # Apply bit quantization: quantize to {-2^(bits-1), ..., 2^(bits-1)-1}\n            # Normalize and quantize\n            max_val = 2**(bits - 1) - 1\n            \n            X_train_quant = np.round(X_train_sparse / (np.abs(X_train_sparse).max() + 1e-8) * max_val).astype(np.float64)\n            X_test_quant = np.round(X_test_sparse / (np.abs(X_test_sparse).max() + 1e-8) * max_val).astype(np.float64)\n            \n            # Train model on sparse + quantized data\n            model = LogisticRegression(C=1.0/10.0, solver='lbfgs', max_iter=1000)\n            model.fit(X_train_quant, y_train)\n            y_pred_proba = model.predict_proba(X_test_quant)[:, 1]\n            brier = brier_score_loss(y_test, y_pred_proba)\n            config_scores.append(brier)\n        \n        mean_score = np.mean(config_scores)\n        results_config[(sparsity, bits)] = mean_score\n        print(f\"Sparsity {sparsity}% + {bits} bits: Mean Brier = {mean_score:.6f}\")\n\n# Find the configuration that retains the most dense-model performance\n# (i.e., has the closest score to the dense baseline)\nbest_config = None\nbest_performance_retention = -np.inf\n\nfor config, score in results_config.items():\n    # How close to dense baseline? 1) - 1\n            \n            X_train_quant = np.round(X_train_sparse / (np.abs(X_train_sparse).max() + 1e-8) * max_val).astype(np.float64)\n            X_test_quant = np.round(X_test_sparse / (np.abs(X_test_sparse).max() + 1e-8) * max_val).astype(np.float64)\n            \n            # Train model on sparse + quantized data\n            model = LogisticRegression(C=1.0/10.0, solver='lbfgs', max_iter=1000)\n            model.fit(X_train_quant, y_train)\n            y_pred_proba = model.predict_proba(X_test_quant)[:, 1]\n            brier = brier_score_loss(y_test, y_pred_proba)\n            config_scores.append(brier)\n        \n        mean_score = np.mean(config_scores)\n        results_config[(sparsity, bits)] = mean_score\n        print(f\"Sparsity {sparsity}% + {bits} bits: Mean Brier = {mean_score:.6f}\")\n\n# Find the configuration that retains the most dense-model performance\n# (i.e., has the closest score to the dense baseline)\nbest_config = None\nbest_performance_retention = -np.inf\n\nfor config, score in results_config.items():\n    # How close to dense baseline? (lower is better, so we want smallest difference)\n    difference = abs(score - dense_baseline)\n    retention = -difference  # Negate to maximize retention\n    \n    if retention > best_performance_retention:\n        best_performance_retention = retention\n        best_config = config\n\nsparsity_best, bits_best = best_config\nprint(f\"\\nBest configuration: {sparsity_best}% sparsity + {bits_best} bits\")\nprint(f\"Brier score: {results_config[best_config]:.6f}\")\nprint(f\"Dense baseline: {dense_baseline:.6f}\")\nprint(f\"Difference: {abs(results_config[best_config] - dense_baseline):.6f}\")\n"},"partialResult":{"content":[{"type":"text","text":"\n"}],"details":{"status":"ok"}}}

sparsity) / 100)\n            \n            # Select top features based on absolute weight magnitude from a quick model\n            model_quick = LogisticRegression(C=1.0/10.0, solver='lbfgs', max_iter=1000)\n            model_quick.fit(X_train, y_train)\n            feature_importance = np.abs(model_quick.coef_[0])\n            top_features = np.argsort(feature_importance)[-n_keep:]\n            \n            X_train_sparse = X_train[:, top_features]\n            X_test_sparse = X_test[:, top_features]\n            \n            # Apply bit quantization: quantize to {-2^(bits-1), ..., 2^(bits-1)-1}\n            # Normalize and quantize\n            max_val = 2**(bits - 1) - 1\n            \n            X_train_quant = np.round(X_train_sparse / (np.abs(X_train_sparse).max() + 1e-8) * max_val).astype(np.float64)\n            X_test_quant = np.round(X_test_sparse / (np.abs(X_test_sparse).max() + 1e-8) * max_val).astype(np.float64)\n            \n            # Train model on sparse + quantized data\n            model = LogisticRegression(C=1.0/10.0, solver='lbfgs', max_iter=1000)\n            model.fit(X_train_quant, y_train)\n            y_pred_proba = model.predict_proba(X_test_quant)[:, 1]\n            brier = brier_score_loss(y_test, y_pred_proba)\n            config_scores.append(brier)\n        \n        mean_score = np.mean(config_scores)\n        results_config[(sparsity, bits)] = mean_score\n        print(f\"Sparsity {sparsity}% + {bits} bits: Mean Brier = {mean_score:.6f}\")\n\n# Find the configuration that retains the most dense-model performance\n# (i.e., has the closest score to the dense baseline)\nbest_config = None\nbest_performance_retention = -np.inf\n\nfor config, score in results_config.items():\n    # How close to dense baseline? (lower is better, so we want smallest difference)\n    difference = abs(score - dense_baseline)\n    retention = -difference  # Negate to maximize retention\n    \n    if retention > best_performance_retention:\n        best_performance_retention = retention\n        best_config = config\n\nsparsity_best, bits_best = best_config\nprint(f\"\\nBest configuration: {sparsity_best}% sparsity + {bits_best} bits\")\nprint(f\"Brier score: {results_config[best_config]:.6f}\")\nprint(f\"Dense baseline: {dense_baseline:.6f}\")\nprint(f\"Difference: {abs(results_config[best_config] - dense_baseline):.6f}\")\n"},"partialResult":{"content":[{"type":"text","text":"Sparsity 40% + 8 bits: Mean Brier = 0.302519"}],"details":{"status":"ok"}}}

(lower is better, so we want smallest difference)\n    difference = abs(score - dense_baseline)\n    retention = -difference  # Negate to maximize retention\n    \n    if retention > best_performance_retention:\n        best_performance_retention = retention\n        best_config = config\n\nsparsity_best, bits_best = best_config\nprint(f\"\\nBest configuration: {sparsity_best}% sparsity + {bits_best} bits\")\nprint(f\"Brier score: {results_config[best_config]:.6f}\")\nprint(f\"Dense baseline: {dense_baseline:.6f}\")\nprint(f\"Difference: {abs(results_config[best_config] - dense_baseline):.6f}\")\n"},"partialResult":{"content":[{"type":"text","text":"Sparsity 60% + 8 bits: Mean Brier = 0.287669"}],"details":{"status":"ok"}}}

1) - 1\n            \n            X_train_quant = np.round(X_train_sparse / (np.abs(X_train_sparse).max() + 1e-8) * max_val).astype(np.float64)\n            X_test_quant = np.round(X_test_sparse / (np.abs(X_test_sparse).max() + 1e-8) * max_val).astype(np.float64)\n            \n            # Train model on sparse + quantized data\n            model = LogisticRegression(C=1.0/10.0, solver='lbfgs', max_iter=1000)\n            model.fit(X_train_quant, y_train)\n            y_pred_proba = model.predict_proba(X_test_quant)[:, 1]\n            brier = brier_score_loss(y_test, y_pred_proba)\n            config_scores.append(brier)\n        \n        mean_score = np.mean(config_scores)\n        results_config[(sparsity, bits)] = mean_score\n        print(f\"Sparsity {sparsity}% + {bits} bits: Mean Brier = {mean_score:.6f}\")\n\n# Find the configuration that retains the most dense-model performance\n# (i.e., has the closest score to the dense baseline)\nbest_config = None\nbest_performance_retention = -np.inf\n\nfor config, score in results_config.items():\n    # How close to dense baseline?
